- **cometchat-android-v6-compose-customization**: Make the CometChat Android v6 UI match your app in the Jetpack Compose cohort — theming with the CometChatTheme composable and lightColorScheme/darkColorScheme, per-component style data classes, @Composable view slots, state views, menu options, text formatters, custom message rendering with bubble factories, localization and sounds.
